Quotient.lift : {α : Sort u} →
  {β : Sort v} →
    {s : Setoid α} → (f : α → β) → (∀ (a b : α), instHasEquivOfSetoid.Equiv a b → Eq (f a) (f b)) → Quotient s → β
Quotient.liftOn : {α : Sort u} →
  {β : Sort v} →
    {s : Setoid α} → Quotient s → (f : α → β) → (∀ (a b : α), instHasEquivOfSetoid.Equiv a b → Eq (f a) (f b)) → β
Quotient.liftOn₂ : {α : Sort uA} →
  {β : Sort uB} →
    {φ : Sort uC} →
      {s₁ : Setoid α} →
        {s₂ : Setoid β} →
          Quotient s₁ →
            Quotient s₂ →
              (f : α → β → φ) →
                (∀ (a₁ : α) (b₁ : β) (a₂ : α) (b₂ : β),
                    instHasEquivOfSetoid.Equiv a₁ a₂ → instHasEquivOfSetoid.Equiv b₁ b₂ → Eq (f a₁ b₁) (f a₂ b₂)) →
                  φ
Quotient.lift₂ : {α : Sort uA} →
  {β : Sort uB} →
    {φ : Sort uC} →
      {s₁ : Setoid α} →
        {s₂ : Setoid β} →
          (f : α → β → φ) →
            (∀ (a₁ : α) (b₁ : β) (a₂ : α) (b₂ : β),
                instHasEquivOfSetoid.Equiv a₁ a₂ → instHasEquivOfSetoid.Equiv b₁ b₂ → Eq (f a₁ b₁) (f a₂ b₂)) →
              Quotient s₁ → Quotient s₂ → φ
Quotient.pliftOn : {β : Sort u_1} →
  {α : Sort u} →
    {s : Setoid α} →
      (q : Quotient s) →
        (f : (a : α) → Eq q (Quotient.mk s a) → β) →
          (∀ (a b : α) (h : Eq q (Quotient.mk s a)) (h' : Eq q (Quotient.mk s b)),
              instHasEquivOfSetoid.Equiv a b → Eq (f a h) (f b h')) →
            β
Quotient.recOnSubsingleton : {α : Sort u} →
  {s : Setoid α} →
    {motive : Quotient s → Sort v} →
      [h : ∀ (a : α), Subsingleton (motive (Quotient.mk s a))] →
        (q : Quotient s) → ((a : α) → motive (Quotient.mk s a)) → motive q
Quotient.recOnSubsingleton₂ : {α : Sort uA} →
  {β : Sort uB} →
    {s₁ : Setoid α} →
      {s₂ : Setoid β} →
        {motive : Quotient s₁ → Quotient s₂ → Sort uC} →
          [s : ∀ (a : α) (b : β), Subsingleton (motive (Quotient.mk s₁ a) (Quotient.mk s₂ b))] →
            (q₁ : Quotient s₁) →
              (q₂ : Quotient s₂) → ((a : α) → (b : β) → motive (Quotient.mk s₁ a) (Quotient.mk s₂ b)) → motive q₁ q₂
Rat.maybeNormalize : (num : Int) →
  (den g : Nat) →
    Int.instDvd.dvd g.cast num →
      Nat.instDvd.dvd g den →
        Ne (instHDiv.hDiv den g) 0 → (instHDiv.hDiv num g.cast).natAbs.Coprime (instHDiv.hDiv den g) → Rat
Rat.mk' : (num : Int) →
  (den : Nat) →
    autoParam (Ne den 0) Rat.den_nz._autoParam → autoParam (num.natAbs.Coprime den) Rat.reduced._autoParam → Rat
Rat.normalize : Int → (den : optParam Nat 1) → autoParam (Ne den 0) Rat.normalize._auto_1 → Rat

      {x_1 : Hashable α} → [LawfulBEq α] → [(k : α) → BEq (β k)] → Std.DHashMap α β → Std.DHashMap α β → Bool
Std.DHashMap.get : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} →
    {x : BEq α} →
      {x_1 : Hashable α} → [LawfulBEq α] → (m : Std.DHashMap α β) → (a : α) → Std.DHashMap.instMembership.mem m a → β a
Std.DHashMap.get! : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} →
    {x : BEq α} → {x_1 : Hashable α} → [LawfulBEq α] → Std.DHashMap α β → (a : α) → [Inhabited (β a)] → β a
Std.DHashMap.get? : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} → {x : BEq α} → {x_1 : Hashable α} → [LawfulBEq α] → Std.DHashMap α β → (a : α) → Option (β a)
Std.DHashMap.getD : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} → {x : BEq α} → {x_1 : Hashable α} → [LawfulBEq α] → Std.DHashMap α β → (a : α) → β a → β a
Std.DHashMap.getEntry : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} →
    {x : BEq α} →
      {x_1 : Hashable α} → (m : Std.DHashMap α β) → (a : α) → Std.DHashMap.instMembership.mem m a → (a : α) × β a

  {β : α → Type v} →

Std.DHashMap.getThenInsertIfNew? : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} →
    {x : BEq α} →
      {x_1 : Hashable α} → [LawfulBEq α] → Std.DHashMap α β → (a : α) → β a → Prod (Option (β a)) (Std.DHashMap α β)
Std.DHashMap.instBEqOfLawfulBEq : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} → {x : BEq α} → {x_1 : Hashable α} → [LawfulBEq α] → [(k : α) → BEq (β k)] → BEq (Std.DHashMap α β)
Std.DHashMap.instDecidableEquivOfLawfulBEq : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} →
    [inst : BEq α] →
      [LawfulBEq α] →
        [inst_2 : Hashable α] →
          [inst_3 : (k : α) → BEq (β k)] →
            [∀ (k : α), LawfulBEq (β k)] → (m₁ m₂ : Std.DHashMap α β) → Decidable (m₁.Equiv m₂)

  {β : α → Type v} →
    {x : BEq α} → {x_1 : Hashable α} → [LawfulBEq α] → Std.DHashMap α β → (a : α) → (β a → β a) → Std.DHashMap α β

Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Impl.ExplorationStep.eq.elim : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} →
    [inst : Ord α] →
      {k : α → Ordering} →
        {motive : Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Impl.ExplorationStep α β k → Sort u_1} →
          (t : Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Impl.ExplorationStep α β k) →
            Eq t.ctorIdx 1 →
              ((a : List ((a : α) × β a)) →
                  (a_1 : Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Cell α β k) →
                    (a_2 : List ((a : α) × β a)) → motive (Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Impl.ExplorationStep.eq a a_1 a_2)) →
                motive t
Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Impl.ExplorationStep.gt : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} →
    [inst : Ord α] →
      {k : α → Ordering} →
        List ((a : α) × β a) → (a : α) → Eq (k a) Ordering.gt → β a → Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Impl.ExplorationStep α β k
Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Impl.ExplorationStep.gt.elim : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} →
    [inst : Ord α] →
      {k : α → Ordering} →
        {motive : Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Impl.ExplorationStep α β k → Sort u_1} →
          (t : Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Impl.ExplorationStep α β k) →
            Eq t.ctorIdx 2 →
              ((a : List ((a : α) × β a)) →
                  (a_1 : α) →
                    (a_2 : Eq (k a_1) Ordering.gt) →
                      (a_3 : β a_1) → motive (Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Impl.ExplorationStep.gt a a_1 a_2 a_3)) →
                motive t
Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Impl.ExplorationStep.lt : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} →
    [inst : Ord α] →
      {k : α → Ordering} →
        (a : α) → Eq (k a) Ordering.lt → β a → List ((a : α) × β a) → Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Impl.ExplorationStep α β k
Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Impl.ExplorationStep.lt.elim : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} →
    [inst : Ord α] →
      {k : α → Ordering} →
        {motive : Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Impl.ExplorationStep α β k → Sort u_1} →
          (t : Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Impl.ExplorationStep α β k) →
            Eq t.ctorIdx 0 →
              ((a : α) →
                  (a_1 : Eq (k a) Ordering.lt) →
                    (a_2 : β a) →
                      (a_3 : List ((a : α) × β a)) →
                        motive (Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Impl.ExplorationStep.lt a a_1 a_2 a_3)) →
                motive t

  {β : α → Type v} →

      [inst : Ord α] →
        (k : α → Ordering) →

                (c : Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Cell α β k) →
                  (Eq (Std.DTreeMap.Internal.Impl.contains' k l) Bool.true → Eq c.contains Bool.true) →
                    List ((a : α) × β a) → δ) →

      [Std.LawfulEqCmp cmp] → Std.DTreeMap α β cmp → (a : α) → β a → Prod (Option (β a)) (Std.DTreeMap α β cmp)

  {β : α → Type v} →
    {cmp : α → α → Ordering} → [Std.LawfulEqCmp cmp] → [(k : α) → BEq (β k)] → BEq (Std.DTreeMap α β cmp)

  {β : α → Type v} →
    {cmp : α → α → Ordering} →
      [Std.TransCmp cmp] →
        [Std.LawfulEqCmp cmp] →
          [inst : (k : α) → BEq (β k)] →
            [∀ (k : α), LawfulBEq (β k)] → {t₁ t₂ : Std.DTreeMap α β cmp} → Decidable (t₁.Equiv t₂)

  {β : α → Type v} → {cmp : α → α → Ordering} → (t : Std.DTreeMap α β cmp) → (n : Nat) → instLTNat.lt n t.size → α

  {β : α → Type v} → {cmp : α → α → Ordering} → (t : Std.DTreeMap α β cmp) → Eq t.isEmpty Bool.false → (a : α) × β a

Std.DTreeMap.mergeWith : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} →
    {cmp : α → α → Ordering} →
      [Std.LawfulEqCmp cmp] →
        ((a : α) → β a → β a → β a) → Std.DTreeMap α β cmp → Std.DTreeMap α β cmp → Std.DTreeMap α β cmp

  {β : α → Type v} → {cmp : α → α → Ordering} → (t : Std.DTreeMap α β cmp) → Eq t.isEmpty Bool.false → (a : α) × β a

      [Std.LawfulEqCmp cmp] → Std.DTreeMap α β cmp → (a : α) → (β a → β a) → Std.DTreeMap α β cmp

              Std.ExtDHashMap α β → Std.ExtDHashMap α β → γ

                γ

  {β : α → Type v} →
    {cmp : α → α → Ordering} →
      {δ : Type w} → [Std.TransCmp cmp] → (δ → (a : α) → β a → δ) → δ → Std.ExtDTreeMap α β cmp → δ

  {β : α → Type v} →
    {cmp : α → α → Ordering} →
      {δ : Type w} →
        {m : Type w → Type w₂} →
          [inst : Monad m] →
            [LawfulMonad m] → [Std.TransCmp cmp] → (δ → (a : α) → β a → m δ) → δ → Std.ExtDTreeMap α β cmp → m δ

  {β : α → Type v} →
    {cmp : α → α → Ordering} →
      {δ : Type w} → [Std.TransCmp cmp] → ((a : α) → β a → δ → δ) → δ → Std.ExtDTreeMap α β cmp → δ

  {β : α → Type v} →
    {cmp : α → α → Ordering} →
      {δ : Type w} →
        {m : Type w → Type w₂} →
          [inst : Monad m] →
            [LawfulMonad m] → [Std.TransCmp cmp] → ((a : α) → β a → δ → m δ) → δ → Std.ExtDTreeMap α β cmp → m δ

  {β : α → Type v} →
    {cmp : α → α → Ordering} →
      {δ : Type w} →
        {m : Type w → Type w₂} →
          [inst : Monad m] →
            [LawfulMonad m] →
              [Std.TransCmp cmp] → ((a : α) → β a → δ → m (ForInStep δ)) → δ → Std.ExtDTreeMap α β cmp → m δ

  {β : α → Type v} →

      [Std.TransCmp cmp] →
        {ρ : Type u_1} → [ForIn Id ρ ((a : α) × β a)] → Std.ExtDTreeMap α β cmp → ρ → Std.ExtDTreeMap α β cmp
Std.ExtDTreeMap.instBEqOfLawfulEqCmpOfTransCmp : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} →
    {cmp : α → α → Ordering} →
      [Std.LawfulEqCmp cmp] → [Std.TransCmp cmp] → [(k : α) → BEq (β k)] → BEq (Std.ExtDTreeMap α β cmp)
Std.ExtDTreeMap.instDecidableEqOfTransCmpOfLawfulEqCmpOfLawfulBEq : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} →
    {cmp : α → α → Ordering} →
      [Std.TransCmp cmp] →
        [Std.LawfulEqCmp cmp] →
          [inst : (k : α) → BEq (β k)] → [∀ (k : α), LawfulBEq (β k)] → DecidableEq (Std.ExtDTreeMap α β cmp)
Std.ExtDTreeMap.instDecidableMem : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} →
    {cmp : α → α → Ordering} →
      [inst : Std.TransCmp cmp] →
        {m : Std.ExtDTreeMap α β cmp} → {a : α} → Decidable (Std.ExtDTreeMap.instMembershipOfTransCmp.mem m a)
Std.ExtDTreeMap.instForInSigmaOfTransCmpOfLawfulMonad : {α : Type u} →
  {β : α → Type v} →
    {cmp : α → α → Ordering} →
      {m : Type w → Type w₂} →
        [Std.TransCmp cmp] → [inst : Monad m] → [LawfulMonad m] → ForIn m (Std.ExtDTreeMap α β cmp) ((a : α) × β a)

  {β : α → Type v} →
    {cmp : α → α → Ordering} →
      {m : Type w → Type w₂} →
        [Std.TransCmp cmp] → [inst : Monad m] → [LawfulMonad m] → ForM m (Std.ExtDTreeMap α β cmp) ((a : α) × β a)

      {γ : Sort w} →
        (f : Std.DTreeMap α β cmp → γ) →

            (f : Std.DTreeMap α β cmp → Std.DTreeMap α β cmp → γ) →
              (∀ (a b c d : Std.DTreeMap α β cmp), a.Equiv c → b.Equiv d → Eq (f a b) (f c d)) → γ

  {β : α → Type v} →
    {cmp : α → α → Ordering} →
      {γ : Sort w} →
        (t : Std.ExtDTreeMap α β cmp) →
          (f : (x : Std.DTreeMap α β cmp) → Eq t (Std.ExtDTreeMap.mk x) → γ) →
            (∀ (a b : Std.DTreeMap α β cmp) (h₁ : Eq t (Std.ExtDTreeMap.mk a)) (h₂ : Eq t (Std.ExtDTreeMap.mk b)),
                a.Equiv b → Eq (f a h₁) (f b h₂)) →
              γ

  {x : BEq α} → {x_1 : Hashable α} → [EquivBEq α] → [LawfulHashable α] → Std.ExtHashSet α → α → Std.ExtHashSet α

              Prod α β

Std.IterM.DefaultConsumers.forIn' : {m : Type w → Type w'} →
  {α β : Type w} →
    [inst : Std.Iterator α m β] →
      {n : Type x → Type x'} →
        [Monad n] →
          ((γ : Type w) → (δ : Type x) → (γ → n δ) → m γ → n δ) →
            (γ : Type x) →
              (PlausibleForInStep : β → γ → ForInStep γ → Prop) →
                (it : Std.IterM m β) →
                  γ →
                    (P : β → Prop) →
                      (∀ (b : β), it.IsPlausibleIndirectOutput b → P b) →
                        ((b : β) → P b → (c : γ) → n (Subtype (PlausibleForInStep b c))) → n γ
Std.IterM.DefaultConsumers.forIn'.wf : {m : Type w → Type w'} →
  {α β : Type w} →
    [inst : Std.Iterator α m β] →
      {n : Type x → Type x'} →
        [Monad n] →
          ((γ : Type w) → (δ : Type x) → (γ → n δ) → m γ → n δ) →
            (γ : Type x) →
              (PlausibleForInStep : β → γ → ForInStep γ → Prop) →
                Std.IteratorLoop.WellFounded α m PlausibleForInStep →
                  (it : Std.IterM m β) →
                    γ →
                      (P : β → Prop) →
                        (∀ (b : β), it.IsPlausibleIndirectOutput b → P b) →
                          ((b : β) → P b → (c : γ) → n (Subtype (PlausibleForInStep b c))) → n γ

  [inst : Monad m] →
    [LawfulMonad m] →
      {β α₁ α₂ : Type w} → [Std.Iterator α₁ m β] → [Std.Iterator α₂ m β] → Std.IterM m β → Std.IterM m β → Prop

    {β : Type u_1} → [Std.Iterator α m β] → [inst : Monad m] → [LawfulMonad m] → Std.IterM m β → Type u_1

  {m : Type u_1 → Type u_2} →
    {β : Type u_1} →
      [inst : Std.Iterator α m β] →
        [inst_1 : Monad m] →

    {β : Type u_1} →

        [inst_1 : Monad m] →
          [inst_2 : LawfulMonad m] →
            {it : Std.IterM m β} → (Subtype fun s => Exists fun s' => Eq s s'.val.bundledQuotient) → it.QuotStep

              {ita : Std.IterM m β} → {itb : Std.IterM m β} → ita.Equiv itb → ita.QuotStep → itb.QuotStep

Std.PRange.UpwardEnumerable.instLETransOfLawfulUpwardEnumerableLE : {α : Type u} →
  [inst : LE α] →
    [inst_1 : Std.PRange.UpwardEnumerable α] →
      [Std.PRange.LawfulUpwardEnumerable α] →
        [Std.PRange.LawfulUpwardEnumerableLE α] →
          Trans (fun x1 x2 => inst.le x1 x2) (fun x1 x2 => inst.le x1 x2) fun x1 x2 => inst.le x1 x2

          {hidx : instLTNat.lt idx decls.size} →
            Std.Sat.AIG.RelabelNat.State α decls idx →
              Eq (Array.instGetElemNatLtSize.getElem decls idx hidx) Std.Sat.AIG.Decl.false →
                Std.Sat.AIG.RelabelNat.State α decls (instHAdd.hAdd idx 1)
Std.Sat.AIG.RelabelNat.State.addGate : {α : Type} →
  [inst : DecidableEq α] →
    [inst_1 : Hashable α] →
      {idx : Nat} →
        {decls : Array (Std.Sat.AIG.Decl α)} →
          {hidx : instLTNat.lt idx decls.size} →
            Std.Sat.AIG.RelabelNat.State α decls idx →
              (lhs rhs : Std.Sat.AIG.Fanin) →
                Eq (Array.instGetElemNatLtSize.getElem decls idx hidx) (Std.Sat.AIG.Decl.gate lhs rhs) →
                  Std.Sat.AIG.RelabelNat.State α decls (instHAdd.hAdd idx 1)

    (num : Int) → Int.instLTInt.lt 0 num → Std.Time.Internal.Bounded.LE 0 (instHSub.hSub num 1)
